No roofs, no ships, no experimental apparatus.\"","\"You make an extravagant promise.\"","\"I have selected a small garden.\"","The garden lay behind an old public library, enclosed by a brick wall and protected from the street by a row of flowering trees. Cassian spread a cloth on the dry side of a bench. Mara watched him unpack bread, cheese, fruit, and two little cakes bought from the waitress at the hospital café.","\"You listened,\" she said.","\"To what?\"","\"I mentioned those once.\"","He looked up. \"You looked pleased when you mentioned them.\"","There it was again, the unfairly simple way he could disarm her. She had learned to recognize elaborate favors and concealed expectations. She was still learning what to do with someone who remembered which cake she liked.","She ate half of it before asking whether he had canceled their old agreement.","\"The false engagement?\"","\"The one with the divided paper and all the careful terms.\"","\"It expires when either of us wishes it to.\"","\"I should like to stop pretending.\"","He put down his cup. \"So should I.\"","The answer made her unexpectedly shy. \"We may have to tell your aunt.\"","\"I had considered sending her a quantitative summary.\"","\"One inconvenient woman, one regrettably willing man?\"","\"An encouraging ratio.\"","Mara leaned against him, laughing. Above the wall, a tourist bus moved slowly past the library. Somewhere nearby, a child was learning a tune on the piano. He had reached the same wrong note three times and approached it again with admirable hope.","For a little while, nothing happened that required her to be clever.","Then Cassian's telephone rang.","She closed her eyes. \"We had nine minutes.\"","\"Eleven.\"","\"Your watch has become generous.\"","It was Noor. The city's main laboratory had no independent samples from the district in which most of the nineteen marked patients had lived. The conservatory station had performed the local tests for years. Its report had repeatedly described the water as stable.","\"The monitors may be wrong,\" Noor said. \"Or the problem may be elsewhere. I want samples that have not passed through their system.\"","Mara looked down at the basket. \"From the household taps?\"","\"Among other places. We will plan the testing with the laboratory. I am calling because the conservatory has refused access to the older records. It claims the archives are closed for a private event.\"","Mara sat up.","Cassian watched her expression. \"You know something about private events.\"","\"I have occasionally attended one.\"","\"Without an invitation.\"","\"I dislike making a habit of dependence.\"","The event was an evening reception for donors to the municipal gardens. Selene Ardent's name appeared on the invitation. So did the name of the foundation that had financed the hospital's trial equipment. Their presence did not surprise Mara. The floor plan did.","She had seen it before, in a small drawing tucked into her mother's notebook. She had assumed it depicted an old theater: a central hall, two narrow passages, a round chamber beneath the main entrance. Now she recognized the stair beside the service lift and the long line labeled FILTER GALLERY.","The theater had been a conservatory all along.","They returned to the workshop. Ivo cleared a place on the bench, muttering about the degree to which romance reduced the punctuality of useful people. Mara informed him that his statement lacked an adequate sample. Anika, who was standing beside him with two coffees, developed a sudden interest in the wall.","June spread the printed floor plan beside Elena's drawing. The public rooms corresponded closely. The round chamber did not appear on the modern version.","\"A space they filled in?\" Rowan asked.","\"Or a space they stopped showing,\" Mara said.","She laid the silver flower on the drawing. Its central pin aligned with a small mark beneath the circular room. The alignment was not a secret message. It was a maker's notation, the sort her mother used to mark a point from which measurements should begin.","Mara could almost see Elena's hand moving across the paper. The memory was so vivid that, for a moment, the workshop disappeared behind it: her mother's sleeve rolled up, a pencil held between her teeth, the patience with which she let a child ask the same question again.","Cassian's hand settled at her back. He said nothing.","She found the next page. A small diagram showed a valve, a removable plate, and the narrow space behind it. Beneath the diagram, Elena had written four words.","KEEP THE OLD ROUTE.","Mara read them aloud.","\"A route to what?\" June asked.","\"That is what we will find out.\"","By evening the group had divided the work. Noor and the independent laboratory would organize water sampling. Rowan would trace the conservatory's suppliers. Ivo would compare the old service plan with the visible structure, while Anika checked the power requirements. June would attend the reception as a reporter. Mara would enter as a guest whose invitation Cassian had acquired through entirely respectable means.","\"Disappointing,\" she told him when he handed it over.","\"I can return it and let you climb the wall.\"","\"A considerate offer.\"","He produced a second envelope. Inside lay the restored feather-shaped pin he had lent her before their first public dinner. The clasp had been bent during the island escape. He had repaired it himself, less neatly than she would have, but well enough to hold.","\"You could have asked me,\" she said.","\"You could have repaired it perfectly. I wanted to return something useful with my own hands.\"","She turned the pin over. The tiny imperfection in the joint made her absurdly fond of it.","\"I am keeping this,\" she said.","\"I had hoped so.\"","At the conservatory that night, light shone through the glass roof like a second, lower constellation. Music drifted across the entrance court. Guests passed beneath arches of flowering vines while photographers called for them to turn towards the cameras.","Mara stood beside Cassian at the bottom of the steps. His dark coat was new. Her dress was borrowed. The pin at her shoulder belonged to a future she was only beginning to imagine.","For once she did not feel that the difference in their circumstances required an apology.","\"Ready?\" he asked.","She smiled. \"Try to keep up.\"","Inside, Lady Ardent was receiving the first guests. Beneath her feet, beyond a wall the public plan no longer acknowledged, a pump started running.","The silver flower trembled in Mara's hand."],"questions":[{"type":"细节","stem":"What prompts Mara to compare the conservatory plan with Elena's notebook?","options":["Cassian has established that no records were kept beneath the building.","A familiar layout in the plan recalls a drawing whose round She closed her fingers around it and looked at Cassian. Beneath the conservatory floor, a pump answered with a low vibration. Elena's old plan had called for the old route; the public map had erased it. Tonight they intended to find out why.","Beyond the glass entrance, the city had put a crown of white flowers around the statue of Prometheus. Below it, somebody had dropped a soda cup into the fountain. Mara thought this described Asterfall's environmental policy better than the banner overhead.","\"You look angry,\" Cassian said.","\"A complicated response to biodegradable stationery.\"","The invitation in her hand had dissolved in the evening rain. Its green ink stained her sleeve. She had survived a flooded compartment, a storm, and a particularly unpleasant encounter with a ship's ladder. It seemed unreasonable that a charity reception should finish her.","Cassian took the soft paper and read the remaining address. \"A celebration of recovery.\"","\"Whose?\"","He looked at the bandage near her wrist, then at the plaster above his own eyebrow. \"Potentially ours.\"","Mara caught his collar and drew him down for a kiss. It was brief enough for the nearby photographer to miss and long enough for Cassian to forget the rain.","\"That,\" she said, \"is a celebration.\"","He smiled in the new, unguarded way she had discovered after the storm. \"I have an unusually favorable outlook on recovery.\"","Behind them, June groaned. \"Can you confine the revolution to one kiss per geographical district? I haven't had dinner.\"","Ivo offered her a biscuit from an oversize pocket. \"Solidarity.\"","\"It's wet.\"","\"So was the ocean. We endured.\"","They entered the gallery together. A replica volcano occupied the central chamber, its artificial ridge covered with clay, moss, and an extremely unhappy toy goat. Warm air drifted from an indoor installation representing the effects of climate change. Mara's clothes were still damp; she found the artistic decision notably hostile.","The evening's chairwoman welcomed donors beneath a suspended screen. Selene herself had left the receiving line to attend a meeting about the city's water equipment, a secretary explained. Her brief absence did not prevent the welcome film from showing her face every few seconds.","On screen, Selene stood beside a rural inlet, speaking about threatened species. The camera moved from a fisherman to a salmon swimming through clear water. A graph rose behind her shoulder. Population decline, the caption said, had been slowed through private endeavor.","June's eye narrowed. \"Slowed where?\"","\"In the little part of the graph behind her hair,\" Mara said.","They separated when the applause began. June went to enquire about the database underlying the film. Ivo followed a technician towards the back of the installation. Noor, already inside, waited near a table of fruit with two sealed sample tubes in a padded bag.","\"The laboratory called,\" she said.","Mara forgot the warmth and the donors. The tubes contained the water collected after the island rescue. A faint remnant clung to the glass, barely visible unless the light hit it sideways.","\"The screening found particles outside the expected range,\" Noor said. \"They are not ordinary sediment. The lab wants a second sample from the same supply branch.\"","\"Can they describe them?\" Cassian asked.","\"Rounded shells around an inner structure. Possibly manufactured. We need more than a blurred image before we give them a name.\"","She showed Mara a photograph. The object resembled a bubble caught inside a tiny cage.","Mara felt the familiar leap of hunger that came with a clue. She wanted it to explain everything: the ship, the devices, the lost record, the weeks of being told she had imagined a connection. Noor's expression stopped her.","\"I know,\" Mara said. \"No magical conclusion.\"","\"Good. Now let's find the source before someone empties it.\"","Mara unfolded a copy of Elena's old plan beside the map on the gallery wall. The public map showed the city's water landscape. Blue streams widened through the peninsula, collecting at a reservoir above the old quarry. An auxiliary line crossed the map's lower corner, then vanished beneath a contemporary sculpture garden.","Mara leaned closer. The line had been covered by a printed label advertising a golf event.","\"Elegance,\" she said. \"A whole pipe sacrificed to a sponsor.\"","She lifted one edge. Underneath stood the words EAST INLET: RESEARCH ACCESS. The route corresponded to the line her mother had marked KEEP THE OLD ROUTE, ending at an unlabelled circle beneath the conservatory.","A man in a brass-buttoned coat appeared beside her. His name tag said Colonel Harrow, Foundation Liaison. He smiled at Cassian and failed to acknowledge Mara.","\"We are grateful for your support, Lord Vey. A public appearance after such weather will reassure the donors.\"","\"We're here to see the installation,\" Cassian said.","\"Of course. The adult tour begins in the next chamber.\"","Mara looked at the goat. \"Is this one intended for children?\"","Harrow gave her the patience of a teacher facing a difficult youngster. \"The elementary display explains the water cycle. A more serious scientific presentation follows.\"","\"Does it explain why the research pipe disappears under a golf label?\"","His smile hardened. \"A formatting error.\"","\"Then it's a marvelous opportunity to correct the format.\"","Cassian stood beside her, entirely still. The colonel looked at the two of them and decided, reluctantly, to address them as a pair.","\"The branch serves a horticultural development. Access is temporarily restricted.\"","\"For whose protection?\" Noor asked.","\"Rare plants. Contamination risk.\"","He moved to block the map. Mara let him. She had already photographed the corner, including the awkward label.","A crash came from the model volcano. Its pail of water had overturned, sending a brown stream across the gallery floor. Guests lifted expensive shoes. A little boy began to cry because the toy goat had fallen into the flood.","Mara was moving before the colonel finished his curse. She caught the child's hand, lifted him onto the stone rim of the fountain, and retrieved the goat with a napkin. The water was warm. An unnatural blue sheen clung to her fingers.","Ivo emerged from behind the installation. \"Nobody touch the cable!\"","He shut the control box. The volcano ceased its small eruption. A decorative screen went black, and half the room exhaled.","The colonel demanded to know who had interfered with the equipment. Ivo showed him a scorched connection.","\"Your technician wanted the pump to run through a damaged socket. I declined to provide a live demonstration of negligence.\"","The boy held out his goat to Mara. \"It needs its habitat.\"","She set it on the dry ridge of a fallen landscape panel. \"A survivor.\"","\"It's a vegetarian,\" he informed her gravely. \"It can't eat the fisherman.\"","\"Neither should the adults.\"","His parent laughed, then thanked her. Mara had forgotten that a room could contain ordinary people as well as predators wearing donors' ribbons.","Noor knelt beside the spilled water. With the technician's permission, she took a labelled sample from the installation's feed container. The laboratory tube now held a similar pale remnant.","\"Same source?\" Mara whispered.","\"The supply tag will tell us.\"","It did. MUNICIPAL WATER AUTHORITY. EAST RESEARCH BRANCH. The code beneath it matched the line on the map.","An elderly woman in an oak-coloured coat came to look at the rescued goat. Her ribbon identified her as a celebrated Dutch zoologist. She studied the child's new habitat with an expression of fierce approval.","\"Better than the film,\" she said. \"They make a cage count as conservation and a barren pit count as a landscape.\"","\"You disagree with the project?\" Mara asked.","\"With the description. I've asked to see the long-fished inlet they claim to recover. The restriction has lasted a month.\"","She showed Mara a photograph: an idle fishing boat, a dead bird, a stain curving along the bank. The image was too small to explain the condition of the water, but the woman had been determined enough to commission tests.","\"Mercury?\" Mara asked, recognising the heading.","\"Among various possibilities. I'm open to being wrong. They're presently open only to a denial.\"","The woman's contempt sharpened as she looked towards the screen. \"I used to bring students from Canada here. They could dive in summer, watch a wolf at the wildlife centre, follow a butterfly through the meadow. Now they ask whether the predator is native and receive a reply about competitiveness.\"","Mara handed back the phone. \"That sounds confusing.\"","\"Deceit generally is. It wants to fascinate you without becoming available to judgment.\"","She gave Mara her card. On the back she had written a sample location and a warning: The supposed research inlet feeds the public branch during maintenance.","Mara's reflex was to turn towards Noor. If the branches could mix, the gallery's mistake might have to do with more than one tank.","Harrow returned with a staff member carrying a cleaning bucket. \"That water must be discarded immediately.\"","\"It will be,\" Noor said. \"After the provider confirms what's in it. There was a child beside the spill.\"","For the first time, the colonel looked at the boy rather than the cameras. His face became unpleasantly blank.","Cassian moved between Harrow and Noor, holding the feed container's label where the room could see it.","\"Bring in your safety officer,\" he said. \"We'll wait.\"","The order carried because the adults around them had begun to look worried, not because a title could turn concern into law. A woman from the gallery came forward, asked everyone to step away, and took control of the spill.","Harrow withdrew towards the service corridor. Mara followed his reflection in the glass. As he passed a door, he slipped a folded card to an attendant.","There was a green butterfly on it.","The attendant went downstairs.","Mara touched Cassian's wrist. He glanced at her, then at the disappearing card. Their understanding required no spoken plan.","June occupied Harrow by asking whether public trust counted as an optional feature of the installation. Cassian intercepted a tray before a waiter could drop it into the wet cable. Mara stepped through the service door and down a narrow shaft of stairs.","The air changed at the bottom. It smelled of damp earth, copper, and leaves kept too long in a shut room. A delivery truck waited beyond a grille. Crates of tomato seedlings stood on its platform beside a metal box stamped BIOLOGY UNIT.","The butterfly attendant was speaking into a phone.","\"No, the sample wasn't meant for the gallery. Someone used the wrong feed tank.\"","Mara crouched behind the crates.","\"I don't care what the timetable says. Drain the lower chamber. Today.\"","She reached for her phone.","A hand caught her sleeve.","Cassian had come down the second staircase. He pointed towards a camera above the truck, then towards the maintenance opening at her feet. Mara smiled despite the cold twist in her stomach.","He knew better than to ask whether she intended to go through it."],"questions":[{"type":"scope","stem":"What makes the research inlet a more Mara enjoyed this fact for approximately two seconds, then noticed the black water below it.","\"Lovely,\" she whispered. \"An exclusive entrance.\"","\"A passing fad,\" he said. \"I predict larger doors in the near future.\"","She lowered herself onto a pipe, held her breath against the smell of grease, and slid sideways beneath the loading floor. The attendant's shoes crossed above her head. Water dripped steadily into a shallow drain. Every drop sounded like a porter knocking on a temple door.","At the far end she found a ladder. It led into a storeroom whose only light came from an infrared camera over the opening. Mara stayed in shadow and studied the door's locking fixture.","Her phone buzzed once.","Cassian: Stay put. I have an aversion to finding you through a wall.","Mara: I have an aversion to waiting beside a bucket.","Cassian: We all sacrifice.","She bit her lip to keep from laughing. Above, a motor started. The truck moved away, and the room grew quiet enough for her to hear the camera's small electronic hum.","A minute later the outer door opened. Cassian entered wearing a delivery driver's coarse cloak and carrying a broken fan.","\"Service call,\" he told the camera. \"Your opening mechanism is making a fabulous noise.\"","Mara stepped behind him before the camera completed its turn. He let the fan fall, and its skeleton of bent blades caught the door. They slipped into the corridor together.","\"Where did you get that coat?\"","\"A man who disliked his employer. I paid the cleaning fee.\"","\"Did you overcharge yourself?\"","\"Exceedingly. It was an emotional transaction.\"","They reached a stairway opening onto the street. June waited outside with two takeaway coffees and an expression of undivided disapproval.","\"You departed from the plan,\" she said.","\"There was no plan,\" Mara replied.","\"My principal objection.\"","Ivo had followed the truck on a bicycle. He returned sweating, with its destination marked on his map: a predominantly industrial valley behind the Municipal Water Authority's southern offices. A large greenhouse stood inside the old service compound. On its public website it housed an environmental education project.","\"Seeds,\" he said. \"Fish. Wildlife. Employees who apparently enjoy putting cameras in storerooms.\"","Noor examined the delivery photograph. \"The box came from the research unit. The greenhouse may receive laboratory supplies.\"","\"May?\" June asked.","\"Yes, may. My vocabulary remains distressingly intact.\"","They regrouped at the hospital cafeteria. Ewan, the man rescued from the ship, was resting upstairs. Noor had spoken to him that morning. His recovery would take time; asking him to perform on command would not hasten it.","Mara ate roast chicken with more hunger than elegance. Cassian stole a piece of her bread. She caught his wrist and took it back.","\"You have property,\" she told him. \"Seek food there.\"","\"My property rarely tastes this good.\"","Rowan slid into the chair opposite them. \"Intimately acquainted with your lunch, I see. Should I interrupt?\"","\"You already have.\"","\"Then I shall be proud of my constancy.\"","He brought a public programme for a greenhouse reception scheduled that Friday. A restoration display would celebrate the reuse of historic water fixtures. One exhibit, a scale model of a medieval fountain inspired by Warwick Castle, had broken during transport. Its retailer wanted a local repair before the trustees arrived.","Mara looked at the picture. A tiny stone elephant supported the basin. One tusk had snapped off.","\"I can mend that.\"","\"I rather hoped so. The stylist is panicking. The chief executive apparently believes an incomplete elephant represents poor standards of living.\"","\"Whose chief executive?\"","Rowan turned the programme. Selene Ardent's name occupied most of the lower page.","By late afternoon, Mara had a legitimate service appointment and a box of tools. Cassian would accompany her as an assistant. Ivo had acquired a plumber's access pass through the contractor responsible for the leaking greenhouse roof. June called it a circus with payroll consequences. Noor called it a chance to obtain another sample before the lower chamber was drained.","The plumber whose pass Ivo would use arrived with a folder of unpaid filings. He was a salaried employee of the contractor, he explained, but his employer had begun to treat every weekend as a donation to modernization.","\"My paycheck is ninety pounds short. Their chief executive says it's an educated guess about future deductions.\"","\"I have a recent objection to guesses made with other people's money,\" Rowan said. He offered the man a lawyer's number, not a miracle.","The plumber opened his folder. A replacement flywheel had been rejected because the product code didn't match an out-dated designation. In the meantime, the pump had been patched with a part intended for a much lower pressure.","Ivo's expression changed. \"That patch will fail.\"","\"I put that in the mail twice.\"","\"Then bring the messages. We'll preserve them.\"","The man looked towards the door, suddenly resolute. \"I can't lose this job.\"","\"Neither can you stop a broken pump by being quiet,\" Ivo said. His bluntness made the room flinch. Then he added, more gently, \"You shouldn't have been made the decider.\"","The plumber drew out a second pass. He had reported the problem, and his access had been cut out of the system that morning. The pass still opened the public service entrance. Its other functions had become invisible.","Mara took a photograph of the warning email. For once, the red flag had been raised by the very person management called troublesome.","The compound lay beyond a pond where dormant reeds shivered in the prevailing wind. A kite circled overhead. Beyond the low roof, a hillside forest covered the old quarry. Nothing about the setting suggested the room beneath the gallery.","Mara wore a practical green dress, heavy shoes, and her hair pinned back. Cassian carried the repair case. A guard checked their booking and waved them through a queue of vans.","Inside, the atmosphere was hot and wet. Dew gathered on enormous leaves. A fan stirred the smell of soil, ripe pear, and warm water. Countless insects moved in the vegetation behind fine mesh. In the central pool, fish turned beneath the reflection of a glass sky.","A guide approached with a lovely, exhausting smile.","\"Welcome to a living model of planetary ecology.\"","\"We're here for the elephant,\" Mara said.","The smile faltered, then recovered. \"Of course. This way.\"","They passed a banner explaining how biomass could help cities remain afloat during environmental change. A display asked visitors to compare a bean plant grown in ordinary soil with one raised in a nutrient mixture. A child pressed both palms to the glass, more interested in a small reptile asleep beneath the stalk.","Mara liked the creature's priorities.","The fountain model stood beside a pool. Its broken elephant had been arranged so the missing tusk faced the wall. Mara disassembled the base, examined the fracture, and began to work. Cassian handed her tools without being asked. Once, when she reached for a knife, he put the correct one in her palm before she looked.","\"Careful,\" she murmured. \"Competence is becoming your most dangerous feature.\"","\"I'll try to cultivate a flaw.\"","\"You could stop looking so pleased with yourself.\"","\"That would be dishonest.\"","A woman passing behind them laughed. She wore a blue laboratory coat and carried a bucket of water in each hand.","\"Don't encourage them,\" said the guide.","The woman set down the buckets. \"I'm a geneticist, not a clergyman. Their consciences are safe from me.\"","Her name was Dr. Peta Ward. She supervised the public greenhouse's growing programme and had a persistent headache caused, she said, by the management's definition of efficiency.","\"We have a wildlife display, a corporate reception, and a drain that won't stop howling.
